Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any elementary module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 46. The projective resolution has length at most 639 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to elementary theory, we decompose the problem into 46 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 639. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.
